  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] 
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] df["col"][row_indexer] = value
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] 
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] Use `df.loc[row_indexer, "col"] = values` instead, to perform the assignment in a single step and ensure this keeps updating the original `df`.
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] 
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] See the caveats in the documentation: https://pandas.pydata.org/pandas-docs/stable/user_guide/indexing.html#returning-a-view-versus-a-copy
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py] 
  [E3_iterative_r3×knn×15_fsqn_py]   out.loc[sample].iloc[order] = target_dist
